- [x] **P1-NOTES-A140** Phase 2: Torrent previews: file, remux, partial download
  - **Difficulty**: HARD
  - **Type**: Feature
  - **Context**: Owns: `src/directStream/previews/torrentSource.ts` and its registry line: phase 1's `LocalSource('torrent', …)` over `TorrentVideos` (a `LocalVideos`), with a `LocalVideo` for a torrent file whole on disk (read from its path), for a remux (its init and segments through ffmpeg's `concat:`, whole or growing), and for a file partly on disk that plays as is; each names its home `<hash>/`. For the partly downloaded file: reading only what is on disk, through an in-process 127.0.0.1 gate (`loopback: true`) that serves verified pieces only and answers 416 for anything else; a read that would need a piece that is not there ends the sheet at once as `PreviewNotYet` (never a try used up) instead of waiting or reading a hole's zeros; the byte-to-time map is used only to decide when a sheet is worth trying. Sheets kept in `<hash>/notesd-previews/<file index>/`; `ownersOf` giving them to their episode so a one-episode delete takes them; sizes counting them; the budget clearing and whole-folder delete taking them (already recursive: prove it). Remux only: sheets made from the original stay valid when the original is deleted; a film that lives as its remux gets its sheets from the remux. The sweep for whole torrent files and whole remuxes with no previews (an episode fetched ahead has its previews before it is played). Does not touch: `web/`, `claude/streaming/`, the cache source, the builder and queue of phase 1 (uses them as they are), `src/daemon.ts`, `src/background.ts`, `scripts/docs/httpRoutes.ts`, `passThrough.ts`, the guides' prose. Exit criteria: a torrent session's previews list frames for what is on disk, for all three shapes; no preview code path calls `use`, selects a file or reads through the swarm (a test proves the engine saw nothing); deleting one episode or the whole download leaves no preview file.

- [x] **P1-NOTES-A139** Phase 1: Preview core: builder, store, routes, the cloud-cache source
  - **Difficulty**: HARD
  - **Type**: Feature
  - **Context**: Owns: `src/directStream/previews/`: the types of the contract, the sheet plan (which stretches make which sheet), the ffmpeg sheet builder (spawn injected, niced, time limit, abort, `*.part` then rename, bounded retries), the one-at-a-time queue with the session on screen first, the on-disk index of finished sheets, the cue list from the index, the source registry, the cloud-cache source and its `LocalVideo` (HLS cache and plain-file cache; partial and whole). The builder runs `-xerror`, under `-protocol_whitelist file,crypto,data,concat` (plus `http,tcp` for a `loopback` input, refused unless every address is `http://127.0.0.1`), awaits `cleanup()`, treats `PreviewNotYet` as "not yet" (never a try), and never makes a video's home folder again: before a sheet is kept it re-checks that the video's folder still exists. The cache source answers only a session that plays from that cache (its `cached`); a pass-through session of a title cached here gets none from it. What starts a build: a session on screen whose title has video on disk (looked at again as coverage grows), and a sweep at start and on a timer for whole caches with no previews. `preview_rev` on the server session, in `SessionInfo` and in the position answer; `StreamPreviews.changed`; `DirectStreamService.passThroughFetch` and `PreviewSourceDeps` with `torrents`, `fetch`, `changed`, `log`. The two routes (`GET /api/stream/sessions/:id/previews`, `GET /api/stream/sessions/:id/previews/:name`), their entries in `scripts/docs/httpRoutes.ts`, `npm run docs:gen`. `NOTESD_STREAM_PREVIEWS` in `src/config.ts` (on by default, off in `DEFAULT_DIRECT_STREAM_CONFIG`), its row in `docs/guide/reference/configuration.md`, and in `.claude/skills/verify-notesd/scripts/verify.sh` the one rule every phase's proof uses: off by default, a caller's `NOTESD_STREAM_PREVIEWS=on` passed through. Wiring in `src/deps.ts`, `src/daemon.ts`, `src/background.ts` (start; close before every other closer). The cache's size on disk counting the previews folder, and its deletion taking it along. Exit criteria: with a cache on disk (whole or partly), a session playing that title answers the previews route with frames for exactly the stretches on disk; sheets survive a restart; a failing ffmpeg leaves no file and stops after the retry limit; with the switch off nothing is spawned.

- [x] **P1-NOTES-SP03** Phase 3: A provider's own preview track
  - **Difficulty**: NORMAL
  - **Type**: Feature
  - **Context**: Owns: `claude/streaming/direct/resolve.mjs`: a `.vtt` hit that `isThumbnailTrack` names is no longer dropped without a trace; the source carries `thumbnails: {url, referer, origin, user_agent} | null` (the first such hit; still never a subtitle). Its test. `src/directStream/runner.ts`, `types.ts`: the field through zod into `ResolvedSource`. `src/directStream/previews/providerSource.ts` and its registry line: fetch the track once per session with the captured headers, a size limit and a time limit; parse its cues (a picture address with `#xywh=x,y,w,h`; a cue whose picture has no fragment is never listed, since the contract's cue needs a rectangle and only fetching every picture would tell it); resolve each picture against the track's address; allow the session to fetch the hosts the track named, and only those; list cues whose `url` is the pass-through with `img=1`. Its factory reads `deps.fetch` and calls `deps.changed`. `src/directStream/passThrough.ts`, `evidence.ts`, `delivery.ts`: a request with `img=1` is kind `image`: streamed through, its answer required to be an `image/*` type and under a size limit, recorded as `image` and never counted in a provider's delivery. The pass-through route's entry in `scripts/docs/httpRoutes.ts` (the `img` query) and the `source` sentence of phase 1's previews entry (`provider`), `docs:gen`. `claude/skills/report-stream-bug/diagnose.mjs`: a stream-bug report leaves evidence kind `image` out of the pass-through answers it judges, with its test. A cache made from a session with a provider track: the provider list is used while the stream host answers; nothing of the provider's pictures is saved. Does not touch `web/`, `sniff.ps1`, `sniff.mjs`, the builder, the torrent code, `rewrite.ts`, `src/daemon.ts`, the guides' prose. Exit criteria: a resolve whose page loaded `thumbs.vtt` gives a session whose previews are `source: "provider"` with pass-through addresses; a track that is HTML, too big, unreachable or without picture cues gives `none` and no evidence record of kind `media`; no request leaves for a host the track did not name.

  - **Satisfies**: R3 — DATA MODEL - Three layers: title (movie or series, TMDB data, outlives its files), file (path, host, size, duration, season/episode, first_seen, deleted_at), play events (file, started/ended, position, duration). Studios and people are real rows so "Blumhouse films I saw this year" is a plain query. - Series collapse into one title/detail page with per-episode progress and per-episode deleted flag. - Sync detects new files and deleted files. A move (e.g. Telegram Desktop -> Videos/Done, matched by name+size) is NOT a delete. A move into Videos/Done is a "finished" signal (owner: usually, not always). - Deleted is not the same as watched; derive state from last position: deleted at >= 90% or manually flagged = finished; deleted partway = dropped; deleted at 0% = removed unwatched. Detail page shows a deleted flag. - User state: manual "watched" flag (covers the 95%-skipping-credits case), rating 0-5 stars, optional review. Review is a normal note linked to the title (search, revisions, /n for free) shown as one text field on the detail page; rating is a column. - Files and VLC are per-host: Play works only on the owning machine (available:false / not_on_this_machine pattern, same as photos). · criteria: R3.4, R3.8
